Method: 本文采用logit参数化随机策略，基于Wang et al. (2020)和Jia與Zhou (2022b)的连续时间强化学习框架，构造了一个新颖的Lyapunov函数来证明策略梯度更新的几乎必然收敛性，并推导出非渐近遗憾上界。该Lyapunov函数也扩展到离散时间策略梯度算法的分析。

Result: 证明了在任意常数学习率下策略梯度几乎必然收敛到最优臂；当常数学习率低于时间不变阈值时，遗憾上界为O(log T)；通过新颖Lyapunov函数改进了Lattimore (2026a)的分析。

Conclusion: 利用SDE工具构造Lyapunov函数为策略梯度分析提供了简洁透明的路径，且该工具在连续时间与离散时间设置下均适用。

Abstract: This paper studies the policy gradient update for a multi-arm bandit problem in diffusion environment that is described by a stochastic differential equation (SDE) under the continuous-time reinforcement learning framework by Wang et al. (2020), Jia and Zhou (2022b). With the logit parameterization for the stochastic policy, we show that it converges almost surely to the optimal arm under an arbitrary constant learning rate. Furthermore, we derive the non-asymptotic regret upper bound when the constant learning rate is below a time-invariant threshold; and the regret bound has order $O(\log T)$. We improve the analysis in Lattimore (2026a) for the same SDE by constructing a novel Lyapunov function and demonstrate the transparency of analyzing policy gradient using the tools in SDEs. In addition, the same Lyapunov function is also helpful in analyzing the discrete-time policy gradient algorithm.

</details>


### [122] [The Parts Are Greater Than the Sum: Automated Task Sequencing for Efficient Training of Multi-Policy LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29601)
*Jiajia Tang,Sizhe Yuen,Francisco Gomez Medina,Yali Du,Adam Sobey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大语言模型参数高效微调（PEFT）的优化路径组织框架，通过自动化的多策略PEFT架构，在固定参数预算下将优化兼容的适配路径组织为独立的量化低秩适配器（QLoRA），使异构任务在解耦的适配空间中优化，同时保留兼容任务间的正迁移。在TRACE基准上达到44.78的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PEFT使用单一共享低秩适配器（LoRA）优化异构任务序列时，共享优化空间会导致任务间干扰，造成迁移效果差和灾难性遗忘。现有方法通过增加参数容量或组合多个适配器来提升表达能力，但依然依赖单一共享优化路径，未从根本上解决异构任务的优化冲突问题。

Method: 提出优化路径组织框架（自动多策略PEFT架构）：1) 在固定参数预算下，通过任务分组和任务排序自动组织优化兼容的适配路径；2) 将组织好的优化路径实现为独立的量化低秩适配器（QLoRA）；3) 异构任务在解耦的适配空间中被独立优化，而兼容任务间保持正迁移。

Result: 在TRACE基准上的实验表明，从传统单策略PEFT到多策略PEFT性能持续提升，所提出的自动多策略框架在相同可训练容量下达到最佳性能44.78。结果表明优化路径组织比单纯增加适配器容量对异构PEFT更有效。

Conclusion: 优化路径组织比简单地增加适配器容量更能有效提升异构参数高效微调的性能，为PEFT提供了一种新的解耦优化范式的设计思路。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) commonly adapts large language models using a single shared Low-Rank Adapter (LoRA). This shared optimization space often suffers from interference when adapting heterogeneous task sequences, leading to poor transfer and catastrophic forgetting. Existing approaches mainly improve adapter expressiveness by increasing parameter capacity or composing multiple adapters, yet they still rely on a shared optimization path. In this paper, we propose an optimization-path organization framework for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, implemented as an automatic multi-policy PEFT architecture. Specifically, optimization-compatible adaptation paths are automatically organized through task grouping and task sequencing under a fixed parameter budget. The organized optimization paths are implemented as independent Quantized Low-Rank Adapters (QLoRA), enabling heterogeneous tasks to be optimized in decoupled adaptation spaces while preserving positive transfer among compatible tasks. Experiments on the TRACE benchmark demonstrate that performance consistently improves from conventional single-policy PEFT to multi-policy PEFT, with the proposed automatic multi-policy framework achieving the best performance of 44.78 under the same trainable capacity. This suggests that optimization-path organization is more effective than simply increasing adapter capacity for heterogeneous parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [123] [A Human-Centered Validation of the Explainability-Performance Coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29614)
*Christian Oliva,Luis F. Lago-Fernández*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的EPC评分指标（Explainability-Performance Coefficient的扩展），通过显式平衡特征选择稀疏性与模型性能保持之间的权衡来量化解释质量，并在表格、文本和图像三种模态上进行了实证验证，同时通过与人类解释的对比证明了其与人类认知判断的高度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在高风险领域的广泛应用迫切需要可信的可解释人工智能（XAI），但客观评估解释保真度以及将XAI指标与以人为中心的理解对齐仍是关键挑战。

Method: 提出EPC评分（EPC score），作为可解释性-性能系数（EPC）的扩展，是一种模型无关的度量方法，通过显式平衡特征选择稀疏性与保留模型性能之间的权衡来量化解释质量。在表格、文本、图像三种模态上进行实证验证，并独立与人类基于的解释进行对比验证。

Result: EPC评分能有效揭示网络激活、数据维度与解释器性能之间的操作依赖关系；与人类解释的对比验证表明，较高的EPC评分与人类词汇情感判断和空间视觉标注高度一致。

Conclusion: EPC评分作为一种模型无关的XAI评估指标，既能客观量化解释质量，又能与人类中心的理解对齐，为可信可解释人工智能提供了一种有效的评估工具。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of deep learning models in high-risk domains has intensified the need for trustworthy Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). However, objectively evaluating explanation fidelity and aligning XAI metrics with human-centered understanding remain critical open challenges. In this work, we propose a model-agnostic metric, the EPC score, which is an extension of the Explainability-Performance Coefficient (EPC), that quantifies explanation quality by explicitly balancing the trade-off between feature selection sparsity and preserved model performance. Through an empirical validation across tabular, text, and image modalities, we show that the EPC score effectively uncovers operational dependencies among network activations, data dimensionality, and explainer performance. Furthermore, we validate the EPC score against independent human-based explanations, proving that higher EPC scores strongly align with human lexical sentiment judgments and spatial visual annotations.

</details>


### [124] [When Does On-Policy Interaction Help? Representational Tradeoffs in Value-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29617)
*Luca Viano,Antoine Moulin,Audrey Huang,Volkan Cevher,Philip Amortila,Dylan J. Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交互式在线模仿学习算法OVI，核心发现是：专家交互可以放宽学习器的表示能力要求——只需能够表达专家的价值函数而非其完整策略，即可实现统计高效的学习；同时证明了交互的必要性，即离线算法在仅有专家价值可实现性假设下必须依赖策略类的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习（IL）中的行为克隆（BC）等方法面临复合误差和性能瓶颈，尤其在无法完美表示专家策略的蒸馏场景下。已知两种经验有效的改进手段：交互式查询专家和利用价值函数估计。但这两者的内在机制及相互关系尚未被深入理解，本文旨在探讨交互与价值函数学习对表现能力需求的影响。

Method: 提出OVI（在线价值-交互）算法，一种交互式在线模仿学习算法。其核心思路是：在在线交互过程中，学习器仅需从自己的轨迹中学习，通过线性最大化预言机实现策略生成，而不需要直接拟合专家完整的行为分布。同时配以理论分析表明该算法在专家价值函数可实现性假设下是统计高效的，并提供负结果证明离线算法在相同假设下必然依赖专家策略类的复杂度。

Result: 实验表明OVI在多个基准上均优于离线策略方法（BC）、交互式策略方法（DAgger）和离线基于价值的方法。特别是当学习器网络显著比专家缺乏表达力时，OVI的优势最为明显，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 交互式查询在模仿学习中不仅是工程上的权宜之计，而是从根本上改变了问题的统计本质——它将学习算法的表示能力需求从"实现专家策略"降为"实现专家价值函数"，从而在蒸馏和低表现力场景下获得显著的性能提升。交互的必要性也被严格证明。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL)---training an agent to replicate expert behavior from demonstrations---underpins applications from robotics to language model training. Standard approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC) are known to suffer from compounding errors and performance plateaus, particularly when the learner cannot perfectly represent the expert's policy (as is typical, e.g., in distillation). Two interventions are widely understood empirically to improve performance: querying the expert interactively along the learner's own trajectories, and using value function estimation en route to generating a policy rather than directly fitting the expert's full action distribution.
  We investigate the nature of these improvements and their potentially surprising interplay. Our main finding is that expert interaction relaxes the representational demands on the learner: one only needs a model capable of realizing the expert's value function, bypassing the (often stricter) requirement of realizing the expert's policy itself. Concretely, we introduce OVI, an interactive on-policy IL algorithm that is statistically efficient whenever the learner can represent the expert's value function and computationally efficient given access to a linear maximization oracle. We complement this with a negative result showing that interaction is necessary. Namely, without stronger assumptions beyond expert-value realizability alone, any offline IL algorithm must scale with the complexity of the expert policy class. Our findings bear out empirically. OVI outperforms offline policy-based (BC), interactive policy-based (DAgger), and offline value-based IL methods, with the largest gains when the learner network is substantially less expressive than the expert's.

</details>


### [125] [CENDRe: Concept Extraction with Natural Domain Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29621)
*Antonia Holzapfel,Andres Felipe Posada Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CENDRe是一种面向CNN时间序列分类的概念提取方法，通过两阶段聚类自动确定概念数量，并利用梯度在时域和频域中定位概念，弥补了现有时间序列概念提取方法仅关注时域、预定义概念数量和定位不准确的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN广泛用于时间序列分类，但在关键领域的部署需要理解驱动模型预测的时域和频域模式。现有时间序列概念提取（CE）方法存在三个局限：仅操作于时域而忽略频率特征、预先定义概念数量、生成的定位与模型实际使用的区域不匹配。

Method: CENDRe首先通过两阶段聚类（silhouette-guided aggregation）自动发现概念并确定概念数量；然后通过对比潜在表征与其原型的存在分数（presence score）的梯度来定位每个概念，生成聚焦于驱动概念区域的掩码；随后将这些梯度通过可微的可逆映射（如傅里叶变换）传播，得到同一概念在频域的定位；最后为每个概念赋予相关性评分以量化其对各类别的贡献。

Result: 在合成基准上，CENDRe的表示正确性（representation correctness）与最先进的方法相当，且重要性正确性（importance correctness）显著更高。在真实轴承故障数据上，CENDRe成功提取了驱动模型预测的频率带，定位在故障诊断中常被检查的区域，提供了时域概念提取方法无法提供的模型评估证据。

Conclusion: CENDRe通过同时提供时域和频域的概念定位、自动确定概念数量以及更准确的对齐，有效克服了现有时间序列概念提取方法的局限性，为关键领域的CNN时间序列分类模型提供了更全面、可靠的可解释性证据。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are widely used for time-series classification, but their deployment in critical domains requires understanding the temporal and spectral patterns that drive their predictions. Concept extraction (CE) methods identify such patterns by analyzing representations within the models' latent space. However, existing time-series CE methods have three limitations: they operate only in the time domain and overlook frequency features, predefine the number of concepts, and produce localizations misaligned with the regions the model uses. We address these limitations by proposing CENDRe, a concept extraction method for CNNs. It first discovers concepts by clustering per-timestep latent representations in two stages, where silhouette-guided aggregation selects the number of concepts automatically. Then, it localizes each concept through gradients of a presence score that contrasts the latent representations with their prototypes, producing masks that concentrate on the regions driving the concept. These gradients, propagated through a differentiable invertible mapping of the input such as a Fourier transform, yield localizations for the same concepts in the frequency domain. Finally, each concept receives a relevance score that quantifies its contribution to each class. On synthetic benchmarks, CENDRe achieves representation correctness comparable to state-of-the-art CE methods and significantly higher importance correctness. On real bearing-fault data, CENDRe extracts the frequency bands driving the model's predictions, located in regions commonly inspected for fault diagnosis, producing evidence to assess the model that time-domain CE methods cannot.

</details>


### [126] [GQ-FSL: Green Quantized Federated Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29659)
*Idan Roth,Lutz Lampe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个绿色量化联邦分割学习（GQ-FSL）框架，利用随机量化实现本地协作训练和无线传输的能耗优化，通过非对称精度设置将设备能耗与全局收敛解耦，在满足目标精度约束下最小化系统总能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线边缘部署深度神经网络（DNN）受限于移动设备的严格能量和资源约束。虽然联邦分割学习（FSL）通过将工作负载卸载到边缘服务器来减轻设备侧计算，但连续交换切割层数据和子模型仍会带来显著的系统开销和能耗。现有方法未能有效平衡设备能耗约束与全局收敛性能之间的权衡。

Method: 1) 提出GQ-FSL框架，对本地协作训练和无线传输均采用随机量化；2) 支持客户端与服务端子模型的非对称精度级别，解耦设备能量约束与全局收敛退化；3) 为分割架构建立参数化能耗模型，并在统计异质数据下推导理论收敛界；4) 构建联合优化问题，配置DNN分割点和精度级别，在满足严格目标精度约束下最小化系统总能耗。

Result: 实验表明，相比量化联邦学习和全精度联邦分割学习，GQ-FSL在资源受限设备上实现了大尺度DNN部署，获得了更优的能源效率。

Conclusion: GQ-FSL通过在训练和传输中引入随机量化并合理配置分割点与精度级别，在保证目标精度的前提下显著降低了系统总能耗，为资源受限边缘设备上的大规模DNN部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying state-of-the-art deep neural networks (DNNs) at the wireless edge is severely bottlenecked by the strict energy and resource constraints of mobile devices. While federated split learning (FSL) mitigates on-device computation by offloading workloads to an edge server, this may introduce systemic overheads, while the continuous exchange of cut-layer data, and submodels still incurs significant energy consumption (EC). To address this, we propose a green quantized FSL (GQ-FSL) framework that incorporates stochastic quantization for both local collaborative training and wireless transmissions. Notably, GQ-FSL supports asymmetric precision levels for the client- and server-side submodels, effectively decoupling device energy constraints from global convergence degradation. To quantify these tradeoffs, we develop parameterized energy models for the split architecture and derive a theoretical convergence bound under statistically heterogeneous data. Building on that, we formulate a joint optimization problem to configure the DNN split point and precision levels, minimizing the total system EC while satisfying a strict target accuracy constraint. Ultimately, we demonstrate that GQ-FSL enables large-scale DNN deployment on resource-constrained devices, achieving superior energy efficiency compared to quantized federated learning and full-precision FSL.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [RareSense: Rarity-Aware Similarity Search for Anomaly Retrieval in Transactional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28879)
*Sidahmed Benabderrahmane,Talal Rahwan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RareSense是一个针对稀疏事务型异常数据的稀有性感知相似度框架，通过挖掘最小稀有项集和稀有关联规则，将对象映射为稀有规则配置文件并用加权Jaccard相似度进行比较，在多项基准上取得了最佳检索和排名性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典相似度度量（Jaccard、余弦、Hamming）通过原子重叠比较对象，常被频繁背景属性主导；IDF加权虽然是逐项的，但无法显式表示具有信息量的高阶共现结构。因此需要一种能够捕捉稀有高阶共现模式的相似度框架。

Method: RareSense包含四个步骤：(1) 挖掘最小稀有项集作为中间结构；(2) 推导可靠的稀有关联规则；(3) 将对象映射为稀疏的稀有规则配置文件；(4) 使用加权Jaccard相似度比较对象。规则权重综合了逆支持度、置信度、提升度、结构复杂度和稳定性。理论分析表明IDF加权Jaccard是RareSense在仅含单例规则时的受限特例，其诱导距离在原对象上是伪度量，在相同规则配置文件的等价类上是度量。

Result: 在涵盖网络安全和一般分类域的四个基准族上，RareSense取得了最高的宏平均查询条件检索性能；统计分析确认了显著的整体差异，矫正配对比较表明RareSense优于原子基线。在全局异常排名任务中，RareSense取得最高宏平均性能，并且与多个强专用检测器在统计上相当。增益在工作负载依赖上表现，在异常共享可重复稀有高阶结构时最强。

Conclusion: RareSense证明了稀有高阶共现结构比原子属性更能表征异常数据之间的相似性，为稀疏事务异常数据提供了一种理论上完备且实践有效的相似度度量。其性能和统计结果支持稀有规则方法作为异常检索与排名的有力工具，但效果与任务工作负载特性相关。

Abstract: Similarity search over sparse set-valued data is often dominated by frequent background attributes because classical measures such as Jaccard, cosine, and Hamming compare objects through atomic overlap. IDF (Inverse document frequency) weighting partially reduces this effect but remains atom-wise and cannot explicitly represent informative higher-order co-occurrences. We introduce RareSense, a rarity-aware similarity framework for sparse transactional anomaly data. RareSense mines minimal rare itemsets as intermediate structures, derives reliable rare association rules, maps objects into sparse rare-rule profiles, and compares them using weighted Jaccard similarity. Rule weights combine inverse support, confidence, lift, structural complexity, and stability, so that neighborhoods are determined by shared rare evidence rather than uniform feature overlap. We show that IDF-weighted Jaccard is a restricted singleton case of RareSense, and that the induced distance is a pseudometric on the original objects and a metric over equivalence classes defined by identical rule profiles. Experiments across four benchmark families spanning cybersecurity and general categorical domains show that RareSense attains the highest observed macro-average query-conditioned retrieval performance among the evaluated similarity measures. The statistical analysis indicates significant overall differences, with corrected paired comparisons favoring RareSense over the atomic baselines. The gains remain workload-dependent and are strongest when anomalies share repeatable rare higher-order structure. For global anomaly ranking, RareSense achieves the highest observed macro-average performance while remaining statistically comparable to several strong dedicated detectors.

</details>


### [128] [LLM-Based Generative Retrieval for Snapchat Content Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28895)
*Liam Collins,Jiwen Ren,Donald Loveland,Bhuvesh Kumar,Clark Mingxuan Ju,Xuan Guo,Mo Li,Alvin Hou,Yi Cui,Peng Yang,Jian Wang,Saud Afzal Shafi,Nga Than,Ruiming Lu,Wenfeng Zhuo,Dongheng Li,Lili Zhang,Mingtao Zhang,Jinchao Ye,Vincent Xue,Chunhui Zhu,Neil Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SnapLGR，一个在Snapchat上为短视频推荐打造的基于LLM的生成式检索系统。它通过语义标识符(SIDs)、继续预训练(CPT)以及基于TensorRT-LLM的高效推理架构，解决了预训练LLM作为生成式检索器在生产部署中的核心挑战，并在线上A/B测试中相对TIGER式基线实现了多项指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练LLM具有丰富的语义先验、强大的序列建模能力和良好的扩展性，有潜力成为优秀的检索引擎。但将其转化为生产环境中的生成式检索器面临两大挑战：模型需要学习预训练阶段不存在的内部物品词汇表，以及在严格的延迟和成本约束下生成有效的物品标识符。这些问题亟需系统性的设计和工程解决方案。

Method: SnapLGR围绕三个核心设计构建：(1) 从多模态物品嵌入构建语义标识符(SIDs)，结合基于Personalized PageRank(PPR)的共互动对比学习，改善码本利用率、减少碰撞并注入协同信号；(2) 使用继续预训练(CPT)在监督微调(SFT)之前将引入的SID token落地到模型中；(3) 通过TensorRT-LLM CUDA支持的beam search和去中心化的worker-loop架构实现高效服务部署。

Result: 在线上A/B测试中，相对于现有TIGER式生成检索基线，SnapLGR将View Time提升了0.37%，Time Spent提升了0.09%，Deep Sessions提升了0.18%，Deep Sessions Unique User提升了0.11%。此外，论文在固定tokenizer下分解了离线差距，量化了模型架构、规模和预训练各自的增益贡献。

Conclusion: 成功的生产级SnapLGR部署需要表示学习、词汇表落地以及高效训练与服务三方面的联合设计。SnapLGR的实践表明，通过系统化的方案设计，基于LLM的生成式检索可以在真实工业场景中落地并带来可观的业务指标提升。

Abstract: Pretrained large language models (LLMs) are promising retrieval engines because they combine rich semantic priors, strong sequence modeling capabilities, and favorable scaling behavior. However, turning a pretrained LLM into a generative retriever in production deployment raises several challenges: the model must learn an internal item vocabulary that was absent from pretraining, and generate valid item identifiers under strict latency and cost constraints. We address these challenges through the design and launch of SnapLGR, an LLM-based generative retrieval system for short-video recommendation at Snapchat. The system is built around three main designs. First, we construct semantic identifiers (SIDs) from multimodal item embeddings and enhance them with Personalized PageRank (PPR)-based co-engagement contrastive learning, resulting in improved codebook utilization, reduced collisions, and infused collaborative signal. Second, we use continued pretraining (CPT) to ground the introduced SID tokens before supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on user interaction sequences. Third, we make SnapLGR serving practical through TensorRT-LLM CUDA-backed beam search and a decentralized worker-loop architecture. In a live A/B test, the launched system increased View Time by 0.37%, Time Spent by 0.09%, Deep Sessions by 0.18%, and Deep Sessions Unique User by 0.11% relative to the existing TIGER-style generative retrieval baseline. We then decompose this offline gap under a fixed tokenizer and quantify the gains due to model architecture, scaling, and pretraining. Overall, our deployment shows that successful production SnapLGR requires joint design across representation learning, vocabulary grounding, and efficient training and serving.

</details>


### [129] [TransX: Scaling Transformer-based Recommendation via Behavioral and Serving Stream Crossings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28940)
*Da Xu,Liyan Fang,Divya Venugopalan,Sunny Hsu,Xukai Wang,Rishav Roy Chowdhury,Cindy Liang,Nishant Satya Lakshmikanth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TransX 是一种面向生产的编码器-解码器推荐架构，将推荐任务重构为序列到序列的动作转导问题，显式解耦行为流建模与服务事件建模，并通过近线行为编码的跨注意力来条件化下一动作解码。同时伴以摊销式服务策略（增量行为编码+按请求KV缓存），使服务延迟不受行为序列长度影响，在LinkedIn大规模在线实验中取得了+6.0% CTR和+4.4%转化的提升，在线计算减少约80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业推荐系统广泛采用Transformer序列模型，并将推荐范式化为对统一单体用户序列的下一token预测。然而，将异构数据源（如长期用户行为和实时服务事件）折叠进单一token流会模糊其不同的因果角色和时间特征，导致建模效率低下以及训练和服务成本上升。

Method: TransX采用生产导向的编码器-解码器架构，将推荐重构为序列到序列的动作转导问题。核心设计包括：(1) 显式解耦行为流建模与服务事件建模；(2) 通过近线行为编码与实时服务表示之间的可扩展跨注意力来条件化下一动作解码；(3) 共同设计摊销式服务策略，结合增量行为编码与按请求键值缓存，使服务延迟对行为序列长度不敏感。

Result: 在LinkedIn推荐系统上的大规模离线实验和在线A/B测试中，TransX一致优于最先进的DLRM和序列基线模型，实现显著的CTR提升（+6.0%）和转化增益（+4.4%），同时保持与现有生产模型相当的服务成本；共同设计的服务策略将在线计算减少约80%。

Conclusion: TransX通过在序列到序列框架下显式解耦异构行为数据，并通过摊销式服务策略解决部署延迟问题，为工业推荐系统提供了一种兼顾建模质量与推理效率的有效方案，验证了动作转导范式在大规模生产环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems (RecSys) increasingly adopt Transformer-based sequence models, with an emerging paradigm that frames recommendation as next-token prediction over a unified monolithic user sequence. However, collapsing heterogeneous data sources -- such as long-term user behaviors and real-time serving events -- into a single monolithic token stream that obscures their distinct causal roles and temporal characteristics, leading to inefficient modeling and elevated training and serving costs. We propose TransX, a production-oriented encoder-decoder architecture that reformulates recommendation as a sequence-to-sequence action transduction problem. TransX explicitly decouples behavior-stream modeling from serving-event modeling and conditions next-action decoding on scalable cross-attention between nearline behavior encodings and real-time serving representations. To enable low-latency, high-QPS deployment, TransX is co-designed with an amortized serving strategy that combines incremental behavior encoding with per-request key-value caching, rendering serving latency insensitive to behavior sequence length. Extensive offline experiments and large-scale online A/B tests on LinkedIn's recommender systems show that TransX consistently outperforms state-of-the-art DLRMs and sequential baselines, and delivers substantial CTR lift (+6.0%) and conversion gain (+4.4%) while maintaining serving costs comparable to existing production models where our co-designed serving strategy reduces online computation by approximately 80%.

</details>


### [130] [Don't Contrast the Impossible: Region-Constrained Batching for Contrastive User Modeling on a Local Community Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28971)
*Seungho Han,Byeongchang Kim,Jin Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出区域约束批量采样（RCBS）方法，解决本地社区平台（如Karrot）中对比学习面临的"不可能负样本"问题——即由于地理曝光约束，许多用户-物品组合在现实中不可能发生，却在训练中被当作负样本使用，从而稀释了对比学习信号。RCBS通过构建区域同质的mini-batch，使用户主要与可能接触到的物品进行对比，以可行性负样本替代不可能负样本，提升了用户表征质量和多个下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推荐系统中的标准in-batch负采样隐式假设任何用户都可以看到任何物品（universal exposure）。但在本地社区平台上，曝光受到地理位置的严格约束，许多用户-物品对在设计上就不可能发生，却仍被当作负样本参与训练，这会稀释对比学习的信号，降低用户表征质量。

Method: 提出Region-Constrained Batch Sampling (RCBS)批采样方法：构建区域同质的mini-batch，确保同一批次内的用户和物品来自相同地理区域，从而使用户在对比学习中主要与现实中可实际曝光的物品进行对比。通过用可行负样本替代不可能负样本，该方法自然引入了更困难、更具信息量的负样本，且不改变模型架构。

Result: 通过离线评估和在线A/B测试验证，RCBS能显著提升用户表征质量，并增强首页信息流排序（home feed ranking）、检索（retrieval）和展示广告排序（display ads ranking）等多个应用的效果。最终的用户嵌入已在生产环境中的多个应用中部署。

Conclusion: 将地理曝光约束纳入对比学习的批采样策略，可以有效解决不可能负样本问题，在不改变模型架构的情况下提升用户表征质量和推荐系统整体性能，且该方案已在实际生产系统中得到验证与部署。

Abstract: Contrastive learning is widely used for user modeling in large-scale recommender systems, where standard in-batch negatives implicitly assume universal exposure that any user can be shown any item. On local community platforms such as Karrot, however, exposure is geographically constrained; many user-item pairs are impossible by design yet still treated as negatives during training, diluting the contrastive learning signal. We address this impossible negatives problem and propose Region-Constrained Batch Sampling (RCBS), a simple yet effective batching method that constructs region-homogeneous mini-batches so that users are contrasted primarily against items they could feasibly see. By replacing impossible negatives with feasible ones, RCBS naturally introduces harder and more informative negatives under realistic exposure constraints. With offline evaluations and online A/B tests, we show that RCBS consistently improves user representation quality and consequently enhances home feed ranking, retrieval, and display ads ranking. The resulting user embeddings have been deployed in production across various applications.

</details>


### [131] [Think2Go: Generative Next POI Recommendation with LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28997)
*Zhuang Zhuang,Shanshan Feng,Hangwei Qian,Mingqi Yang,Heng Qi,Yanming Shen,Baocai Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Think2Go是一个新颖的生成式下一POI推荐框架，通过统一监督微调（SFT）和基于强化学习（RL）的推理，在单一架构中联合优化记忆保留和自适应推理，并引入两种优势加权机制（基于核密度估计的提示认知不确定性和基于最大奖励归一化的奖励信息优势缩放），实现隐式课程学习策略，从而增强模型对语义ID（SID）的理解并探索多样化时空模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下一POI推荐方法主要依赖浅层上下文信息和手工特征交互，受限于用户移动模式固有的稀疏性和复杂性，非推理模型难以捕获深层意图；而大语言模型（LLM）在单独训练语义ID（SID）时缺乏对其的深层理解，表现欠佳。

Method: Think2Go在单一架构中统一SFT和RL-based推理，实现记忆与自适应推理的联合优化。具体包括两种优势加权校准机制：(1) 基于核密度方法估计提示认知不确定性，评估查询与用户历史之间的时空周期性模式对齐程度，在高认知不确定性下促进探索；(2) 奖励信息优势缩放，通过除以最大奖励来归一化奖励，自适应调整更新幅度，提升训练稳定性并缓解对噪声信号的过拟合。两者联合形成隐式课程学习策略，提供细粒度、实例感知的策略更新。

Result: 该框架通过联合SFT与RL推理、认知不确定性引导的探索以及奖励归一化的稳定策略更新，实现了防止熵崩塌和鲁棒探索的效果，提升了模型对SID表示的理解能力及下一POI推荐的性能。

Conclusion: Think2Go通过测试时计算扩展（test-time computational scaling）增强了模型对SID表示的理解，并联合校准策略优化形成隐式课程学习，有效应对用户移动模式的稀疏性和复杂性，为生成式下一POI推荐提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation task focuses on mining user behavioral preference patterns from historical check-ins to provide personalized suggestions for the next destination. Existing methods primarily rely on shallow contextual information and handcrafted feature interactions to predict the next POI. However, the inherent sparsity and complexity of user mobility patterns limit the computational capacity of non-reasoning models to capture deep intent, while large language models (LLMs) perform suboptimally because they lack a deep understanding of semantic IDs (SIDs) when SIDs are trained separately. To address these limitations, we propose Think2Go, a novel generative next POI recommendation framework, which enhances the model's comprehension of SID representations and explores diverse spatial-temporal patterns via test-time computational scaling. We unify supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL)-based reasoning within a single architecture, enabling joint optimization of memorization and adaptive reasoning to better retain user behavior patterns while exploring diverse user preferences. To further calibrate policy optimization in adaptive reasoning, we propose two advantage weighting mechanisms that integrate (1) prompt epistemic uncertainty, estimated via kernel density methods to assess the spatial-temporal periodic pattern alignment between queries and user history, promoting increased exploration under high epistemic uncertainty; and (2) reward-informed advantage scaling, captured by normalizing rewards against their maxima to adapt update magnitudes, thereby improving training stability and mitigating overfitting to noisy signals. This joint calibration forms an implicit curriculum learning strategy, delivering fine-grained, instance-aware policy updates that prevent entropy collapse and support robust exploration.

</details>


### [132] [PaletteID: Prototype-Composed Semantic Identifiers for Multimodal CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29000)
*Huanyu Liu,Baining Chen,Hui Liu,Zengyang Li,Ziyi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PaletteID（PID），一种基于原型（prototype）的多模态语义标识符方法，用于替代现有的离散化语义标识符（SID）方法，以提升点击率（CTR）预测精度。PID受调色板配色启发，使用紧凑的原型物品集合作为语义锚点，通过语义质量感知行列式点过程（SQ-DPP）构建原型调色板，并为每个目标物品检索语义相关的原型序列进行聚合，形成信息丰富的PID表示。在两个公开数据集上，PID在CTR预测上持续优于现有方法，尤其对长尾物品有更大增益，且标识符分配更稳健、语义更可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义标识符（SID）的多模态推荐方法存在两大局限：一是码本分配无法保持语义相关性并丢弃了原始嵌入空间中的细粒度连续信号；二是残差码路径高度依赖前缀码，限制了层级标识符的有效表示可扩展性。这些局限导致现有方法在CTR预测上的收益有限。

Method: 首先，提出语义质量感知行列式点过程（SQ-DPP）来构建原型调色板，同时考虑局部内容密度和全局语义多样性。其次，对每个目标物品，检索一组语义相关的原型并聚合成信息丰富的PID表示，实现丰富且互补的语义建模。该设计借鉴了调色板配色思想，用紧凑的原型物品集合作为语义锚点，桥接预训练多模态内容空间与推荐模型。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，PID在CTR预测上持续取得更优效果，且对长尾物品带来更大增益。相比现有残差SID方法，PID生成的标识符分配更稳健，token语义更具可解释性。

Conclusion: PID通过原型化的语义标识符设计，有效解决了现有SID方法在语义保持和表示可扩展性上的缺陷，为多模态推荐中的CTR预测提供了更优的解决方案，尤其对冷启动和长尾问题有显著改善。

Abstract: Multimodal information can improve the accuracy of click-through rate (CTR) prediction and effectively alleviate item cold-start and long-tail problems. Recent studies commonly discretize pretrained multimodal embeddings into semantic identifiers (SIDs), allowing the model to learn task-specific semantic representations for recommendation. However, existing methods still provide limited gains due to two major limitations. First, codebook assignment fails to preserve semantic relevance and discards fine-grained continuous signals in the original embedding space. Second, the residual code paths are highly dependent on prefix codes, which limits the effective representational scalability of hierarchical identifiers. To address these issues, we propose PaletteID (PID), a prototype-based semantic identifier. Inspired by palette-based color composition, PID uses a compact set of representative prototype items as semantic anchors to bridge pretrained multimodal content space and recommendation models. Specifically, we first construct a prototype palette with Semantic Quality-Aware Determinantal Point Process (SQ-DPP), which jointly considers local content density and global semantic diversity. Then, for each target item, PID retrieves a sequence of semantically related prototypes and aggregates them into an informative PID representation, enabling rich and complementary semantic modeling. Extensive experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that PID consistently improves CTR prediction and yields larger gains for long-tail items. PID also produces more robust identifier assignments and provides more interpretable token semantics than existing residual SID methods.

</details>


### [133] [EvoReason: Self-Evolving Reasoning Primitive-Guided On-Policy Distillation for Latent Reasoning in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29010)
*Zhuang Zhuang,Zhipeng Wei,Rongfeng Guo,Shijie Li,Peng Zhao,Jie Chen,Fei Pan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EvoReason是一个自演化的潜在推理框架，通过原语引导的on-policy蒸馏机制，将显式推理监督与学生潜在推理空间自适应对齐，解决推荐系统中直接蒸馏原始CoT轨迹导致的冗余表达和不稳定推理路径问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法直接蒸馏原始CoT轨迹到潜在表示中，假设文本推理轨迹能提供充分监督。但推荐推理轨迹包含多样化的推理过程，存在冗余表达和不稳定推理路径，使原始CoT监督难以学习可迁移的潜在推理表示。

Method: EvoReason包含三个关键步骤：(1)从高质量智能体推荐轨迹中提取可复用的推理原语作为伪工具；(2)基于原语赋予教师模型原语感知推理能力，生成结构化且低冗余的CoT监督；(3)引入自演化的on-policy蒸馏机制，使原语引导的推理过程根据学生潜在推理结果动态演化，形成闭环共同进化。

Result: 通过闭环共同进化机制，策略更新持续根据潜在推理结果优化推理行为，实现渐进式更好的CoT监督对齐和更有效的推理迁移。

Conclusion: EvoReason通过自适应对齐显式推理监督与潜在推理空间的自我演化框架，有效解决了原始CoT监督在潜在推理学习中的次优问题，实现了更高效、更稳定的推理迁移。

Abstract: Generative recommendation benefits from reasoning-enhanced inference, and latent reasoning offers an efficient paradigm by encoding intermediate reasoning processes into compact continuous representations for latency-sensitive deployment. Despite its efficiency, existing latent reasoning approaches typically rely on directly distilling raw chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories into latent representations, assuming that textual reasoning traces provide sufficient supervision. However, recommendation reasoning trajectories contain diverse reasoning processes with redundant expressions and unstable reasoning paths, making raw CoT supervision suboptimal for learning transferable latent reasoning representations. To address this challenge, we propose EvoReason, a self-evolving latent reasoning framework that adaptively aligns explicit reasoning supervision with the student's latent reasoning space through primitive-guided on-policy distillation. First, EvoReason extracts reusable reasoning primitives from high-quality agentic recommendation trajectories, where each primitive captures an essential reasoning behavior and serves as a pseudo-tool for structured teacher reasoning. Then, based on these primitives, we equip the teacher with primitive-aware reasoning capabilities, enabling it to generate structured CoT supervision with reduced redundancy and improved consistency. Finally, during latent reasoning optimization, EvoReason introduces a self-evolving on-policy distillation mechanism, where the primitive-guided reasoning process evolves according to the student's latent reasoning outcomes. Through this closed-loop co-evolution, policy updates continuously improve latent reasoning behaviors is refined according to the resulting latent reasoning outcomes, enabling progressively better-aligned CoT supervision and more effective reasoning transfer.

</details>


### [134] [Reproducing LightMem: Naive RAG Is Just as Good for Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29104)
*Yongjie Zhou,Shuai Wang,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究复现了LightMem记忆管理方法，并与直接检索原始用户话语的Naive RAG进行对比。发现检索器选择是性能变化的主要来源（准确率从58.1%到75.5%），构造的记忆并不总是优于原始话语检索。LightMem仅在严格token预算下表现更好，且Oracle评估显示记忆构造会丢失部分答案相关信息。结论是LightMem提供的是上下文效率的权衡而非普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话代理需要访问早期交互信息，但完整对话历史随对话增长而代价昂贵。LightMem声称在低构建成本下表现良好，但已有研究存在两个局限：依赖独立的构造记忆表征，且仅用一种检索器评估，不清楚结果对检索器选择的敏感性以及记忆构造是否丢失答案相关信息。本文旨在复现并严格对比LightMem与Naive RAG，以澄清这些问题。

Method: 复现LightMem并与Naive RAG（直接检索原始用户话语）进行系统比较。具体包括：在固定LightMem记忆存储上更换不同检索器以测试检索器敏感性；在匹配的检索深度下对比两种方法的答案准确率；在不同token预算下评估性能；使用Oracle评估检查记忆构造是否移除了答案相关信息。

Result: 复现了LightMem的主要配置趋势，但发现检索器选择是性能变化的主要来源：仅更换检索器即可使准确率从58.1%变化到75.5%。构造的记忆并不始终优于原始话语检索——在匹配检索深度下Naive RAG通常表现更好，LightMem仅在严格回答token预算下更优。Oracle评估进一步证明记忆构造会移除部分答案相关信息。

Conclusion: LightMem提供的是上下文效率的权衡而非对Naive RAG的普遍优势。其价值取决于检索器和可用的token预算，这促使未来研究关注检索、重排序、查询构建及其与原始和构造记忆表征的交互。

Abstract: Long-term conversational agents require access to information from earlier interactions, such as a user's preferences, past requests, or previously mentioned facts. Repeatedly providing the full dialogue history can be expensive as conversations grow, so many memory approaches instead transform past interactions into compact entries that can be retrieved when needed. LightMem is a recent lightweight memory-management approach that reports strong effectiveness while maintaining relatively low construction cost. However, it still relies on a separate constructed memory representation and is evaluated with only one retriever, leaving unclear how sensitive its results are to retriever choice and whether memory construction discards answer-relevant information.
  In this study, we reproduce LightMem and compare it with Naive RAG, which retrieves directly from raw user turns. We recover LightMem's main configuration trend, but find that retriever choice is a major source of performance variation: changing only the retriever over a fixed LightMem store shifts answer accuracy from 58.1% to 75.5%. Constructed memories also do not consistently outperform raw-turn retrieval. Naive RAG generally performs better at matched retrieval depths, whereas LightMem performs better mainly under tight answering-token budgets. Oracle evaluation further shows that memory construction removes some answer-relevant information.
  Overall, LightMem offers a context-efficiency trade-off rather than a general advantage over Naive RAG. Its value depends on the retriever and available token budget, motivating future work on retrieval, reranking, query formulation, and their interaction with raw and constructed memory representations.

</details>


### [135] [GALA: Generative Aligned Learning for Adaptive Multimodal Representation in the Taobao Shangou Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29213)
*Jiping Liu,Zhongmin Zhang,Zisen Sang,Zhijia Fang,Tao Ouyang,Ma Jiang,Shaopeng Liang,Zeyang Hou,Guodong Cao,Jia Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GALA，一个三阶段多模态推荐系统流水线，核心创新在于引入"生成式RL对齐"中间阶段，通过基于转换奖励的GRPO优化来弥合图像-文本预训练与行为驱动排序模型之间的差距，已在淘宝闪购生产环境部署，服务超2亿日活用户，在线A/B测试显示订单量提升0.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外卖/食品推荐系统中多模态信号（图像、文本、用户交互历史）的有效融合仍具挑战性。主流两阶段方法将内容语义预训练与行为驱动排序分离，导致语义理解与用户行为模式之间的对齐不足，难以适应不断演化的用户意图。

Method: GALA采用三阶段流水线：（1）行为感知三元组预训练，从搜索日志中构建query-图像-文本对以捕捉早期用户意图和内容偏好；（2）核心创新阶段——通过生成式RL对齐，利用GRPO（基于转换奖励的强化学习）动态优化多模态嵌入，弥合预训练与微调之间的差距；（3）通过自适应门控和混合损失集成多模态与ID嵌入，在长期ID主导训练下保留多模态贡献。

Result: 在淘宝闪购生产环境部署，服务超2亿日活用户。相比SOTA方法，离线指标一致提升（AUC +0.12/+0.20），PCOC指标表现更优；大规模在线A/B测试显示订单量提升0.55%，验证了工业规模下的有效性和对不同需求模式的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GALA通过引入生成式RL对齐中间阶段，有效弥合了多模态预训练与行为驱动排序之间的对齐差距，实现了语义理解与用户行为模式的动态对齐，并在工业级大规模部署中证明了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems in food delivery increasingly leverage multimodal signals, including images, text, and user interaction histories, to enhance user experience, yet effective fusion of these heterogeneous modalities remains challenging, hindering both the joint modeling of multimodal signals and adaptation to evolving user intent. In mainstream two-stage approaches, the separation between content-semantic pretraining of image-text encoders and behavior-driven ranking models limits alignment between semantic understanding and user behavior patterns. To address these issues, we present GALA, a three-stage pipeline whose core innovation lies in an intermediate "generative RL alignment" stage that constructs multimodal pretraining data from user behavior and refines it via conversion-based rewards, effectively bridging the pretraining-fine-tuning gap to align with downstream objectives. GALA comprises three stages: first, behavior-aware triplet pretraining on query-image-text pairs from search logs to early capture user intent and content preferences; second, a novel intermediate stage that refines multimodal embeddings through reward-driven optimization (GRPO) to dynamically align them with user behavior and bridge the pretraining-fine-tuning gap; and finally, integration of multimodal and ID embeddings via adaptive gating with a hybrid loss, preserving multimodal contributions under long-term ID-dominant training. GALA has been deployed in the production environment at Taobao Shangou, serving over 200 million daily active users. Compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, it delivers consistent offline gains of +0.12/+0.20 AUC along with better PCOC metrics. Large-scale online A/B tests further report a 0.55 percent increase in order volume, confirming GALA's effectiveness at industrial scale and its robustness across diverse demand patterns.

</details>


### [136] [RecHarness: A Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness for Self-Evolving Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29241)
*Haoran Ling,Yuecheng Li,Zeyu Song,Jing Yao,Shuwen Kang,Chi Lu,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RecHarness 是一个基于 Bandit 路由的智能体框架，用于自动化推荐模型优化。它将优化过程分为两个步骤：bandit 路由器根据历史验证反馈选择修改方向，LLM 在选定方向内生成具体的优化假设和可执行代码编辑。同时引入 jump-basin 机制应对局部停滞。在多个推荐任务、数据集和模型骨干上实现更稳定的性能提升，并在大型短视频广告平台的 7 天在线 A/B 测试中分别提升 ADVV 2.084%、Revenue 0.534% 和 Exposure 0.559%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐模型的优化仍高度依赖工程师手动迭代架构、目标和训练策略。虽然基于 LLM 的智能体可以自动化这一试错过程，但让 LLM 同时负责选择修改方向和生成具体假设，在有限的实验预算下往往导致搜索不稳定。因此需要将方向选择与假设生成解耦，以提高优化的稳定性和效率。

Method: RecHarness 采用两阶段解耦框架：（1）Bandit 路由器根据历史验证反馈选择下一个修改方向（作为探索/利用平衡机制）；（2）LLM 在选定方向内生成具体的优化假设和可执行的代码编辑。此外，采用 jump-basin 机制，当局部编辑陷入停滞时激活结构跳变（structural-jump）分支，以支持长程探索。

Result: 在多个推荐任务、数据集和模型骨干上，RecHarness 比纯 LLM 推理搜索实现了更稳定的性能提升，并更有效地利用有限的实验预算。在大型短视频广告平台上为期 7 天的在线 A/B 测试中，所选候选方案使 ADVV 提升 2.084%、Revenue 提升 0.534%、Exposure 提升 0.559%。

Conclusion: RecHarness 通过将修改方向选择（bandit）与具体假设生成（LLM）解耦，并辅以 jump-basin 机制应对局部停滞，有效提升了自动化推荐模型优化的稳定性和预算效率，在离线多个基准和在线大规模广告平台上均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Optimizing modern recommender models still depends heavily on engineers manually iterating over architectural, objective, and training-strategy changes. While LLM-based agents can automate this trial-and-error process, allowing the LLM to both select modification directions and generate concrete hypotheses often leads to unstable search under limited experiment budgets. Inspired by the above challenge, we propose RecHarness, a Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness for automated recommender model optimization. RecHarness separates the optimization process into two steps: a bandit router selects the next modification direction according to historical validation feedback, while the LLM generates a concrete optimization hypothesis and executable code edit within the selected direction. To sustain long-horizon exploration, RecHarness uses a jump-basin mechanism to activate a structural-jump arm when local edits stagnate. Across multiple recommendation tasks, datasets, and model backbones, RecHarness achieves more stable performance improvements and uses limited trial budgets more effectively than LLM-reasoning search. During a 7-day online A/B test on a large-scale short-video advertising platform, the selected candidate improves ADVV by 2.084%, Revenue by 0.534%, and Exposure by 0.559%. Code is available at https://github.com/6lyc/RecHarness.

</details>


### [137] [Language Models Agree With Each Other, Not With Readers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29274)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过一个非为研究目的构建的人类参考基准（2,523个读者标记集，涵盖120个网页文档）来测量语言模型之间的趋同（homogenisation）。研究发现18个模型臂中位模型对一致性为+0.093，远高于人类基准+0.040，其中99对模型完全位于人类区间之上，但该效应并非确定性、提示词或供应商所致，而是可分级（与模型规模相关）。然而在样本外测试中，四个后续发布模型均未超过人类区间，且多个模型模拟出的群体并非多个独立群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于语言模型同质化的研究多依赖为研究专门收集的人类判断作为参照，这使人类侧成为设计的人为产物——众包工人实际是在运行模型的提示词。本文希望使用一个独立于研究目的构建的人类参考基准来客观测量模型间的趋同程度。

Method: 使用2,523个读者标记集（120个网页文档，读者出于自身目的做标注，且默认不显示他人标记）作为人类参考。一致性定义为两个大小匹配的句子集合的重叠减去在各自深度和长度带内重采样时的期望重叠。零假设校准经过实证验证（随机基线的每对值均在0.006以内）。测试了18个模型臂（涵盖11个供应商、3个国家、两种权重体制）的153对模型。

Result: 每个文档中位数上各方命名14/70个句子；两位读者共享4.1句，两个模型共享8.7句。153对模型的中位一致性为+0.093，人类标尺为+0.040，99对模型完全高于人类区间。两个前沿模型达到+0.203，是GPT-4o自一致性的两倍。效应非确定性、提示词或程序所致，且为分级效应：最小模型与人类水平相当。样本外测试中四个后续发布的模型均未超出人类区间。

Conclusion: 模型间的趋同是真实且显著的，超出人类读者之间的自然一致性，但该效应具有规模依赖性（最小模型仅达人类水平），且倍数受程序影响而排序稳定。关键在于样本外验证显示：后续发布模型的趋同未超过人类区间，且由多个模型模拟出的种群并非多个独立种群——即模型群体的多样性是虚假的。

Abstract: Claims that language models homogenise are usually measured against human judgements collected for the study, which makes the human side an artifact of the design: a crowdworker given the model's instruction is running the model's prompt. We measure convergence against a human reference nobody built for the purpose -- 2,523 reader mark sets across 120 web documents, produced by people highlighting for their own reasons on a platform where the overlay of others' marks is off by default.
  Agreement is the overlap between two size-matched sentence sets minus the overlap expected when each is resampled within its own depth-and-length bands. The null's calibration is demonstrated, not asserted: every pair involving a random baseline lands within 0.006 of zero. On the median document each party names 14 sentences of 70; two readers share 4.1 and two models 8.7.
  Across 18 model arms spanning 11 vendors, 3 countries and both weight regimes, the median of 153 model pairs is +0.093 against a human yardstick of +0.040, and 99 sit entirely above the human interval. Two frontier models from rival labs reach +0.203, twice what GPT-4o agrees with itself on a second call. The effect is not determinism, prompt wording, procedure, vendor or routing, and it is graded: the smallest models agree at the human level. No model agrees with readers detectably more than a reader does, and at equal depth and length no surface feature separates their choices.
  The multiples are procedure-dependent and the ordering is not: models are cut to their sharpest set while a reader's is a random draw from what they marked, and blunting the models alike halves the gap without closing it. Tested out of sample on four models released after this analysis, against predictions fixed beforehand, none clears the human interval. A population simulated from several models is not several populations.

</details>


### [138] [Bridging the Question-Answer Gap in Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29402)
*Domen Vake,Jernej Vičič,Aleksandar Tošić*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HyPE框架，将假设性内容生成从查询阶段转移到索引阶段，通过为每个数据块预计算假设提示词并进行嵌入，将检索转变为问题-问题匹配任务，在不增加查询延迟的情况下显著提升RAG系统的检索精度和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中存在用户查询与文档文本之间的风格差异问题，传统的HyDE方案虽能改善对齐但引入了查询时的额外计算开销，需要在不增加推理延迟的前提下解决检索对齐问题。

Method: HyPE框架在索引阶段为每个数据块预计算多个假设提示词，并用数据块的嵌入替代提示词的嵌入进行存储。检索时查询与预计算的嵌入进行匹配，实现问题-问题匹配，避免运行时生成合成答案。

Result: 在六个常见数据集上的实验表明，与标准方法相比，HyPE能将检索上下文精确度提升最多42个百分点，声明召回率提升最多45个百分点，同时与重排序、多向量检索、查询分解等RAG扩展技术兼容。

Conclusion: HyPE通过将假设内容生成从查询时转移到索引阶段，在消除额外查询延迟的同时增强了查询与相关上下文的对齐，是一种高效且兼容性强的RAG检索增强方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems synergize retrieval mechanisms with generative language models to enhance the accuracy and relevance of responses. However, bridging the style gap between user queries and relevant information in document text remains a persistent challenge in retrieval-augmented systems, often addressed by runtime solutions (e.g., Hypothetical Document Embeddings (HyDE)) that attempt to improve alignment but introduce extra computational overhead at query time. To address these challenges, we propose Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings (HyPE), a framework that shifts the generation of hypothetical content from query time to the indexing phase. By precomputing multiple hypothetical prompts for each data chunk and embedding the chunk in place of the prompt, HyPE transforms retrieval into a question-question matching task, bypassing the need for runtime synthetic answer generation. This approach does not introduce latency but also strengthens the alignment between queries and relevant context. Our experimental results on six common datasets show that HyPE can improve retrieval context precision by up to 42 percentage points and claim recall by up to 45 percentage points, compared to standard approaches, while remaining compatible with re-ranking, multi-vector retrieval, query decomposition, and other RAG advancements

</details>


### [139] [QASP: Query-Adaptive Robust Vector Search Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29606)
*Hakan Ferhatosmanoglu,Kushal Kumar,Tal Wagner,Andy Warfield*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: QASP（查询自适应鲁棒向量搜索策略）通过一次性有监督回归预测每条查询的完整召回进度曲线，为任意召回目标推导搜索策略，避免了迭代模型调用和独立目标预测器。理论上证明其训练样本有限性、损失渐近优势及数据访问节省随内在维度指数增长；实验上在保证99%召回率的同时减少80%数据访问，并显著降低召回方差、提高查询满意度，且无需重训练即可扩展至大规模数据和层次索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量搜索面临两个核心挑战：固定搜索参数导致不同查询间的性能差异显著（高方差），而传统基于平均召回率的评估掩盖了这种逐查询的不一致性。现有方法要么在搜索过程中迭代调用模型（开销大），要么需要为每个召回目标单独训练预测器（不灵活）。因此需要一个既能适应查询差异、又高效且可泛化的搜索策略。

Method: QASP的核心方法包括：(1) 通过一次前期的有监督回归预测每条查询的完整召回进度曲线（归一化召回值），而非仅预测单点召回率；(2) 使用尺度不变特征和搜索前推理（pre-search inference）实现跨召回目标、索引配置和数据集的泛化；(3) 基于细粒度进度预测，引入轻量级反应式补充机制，依据预测与观察的偏差动态调整搜索深度，无需额外推理开销；(4) 理论上证明其有限训练样本需求、与固定策略下界相比可忽略的损失差距，以及相对于固有维度呈指数增长的数据访问节省。

Result: 实验结果表明，QASP在以下方面显著优于现有方法：召回方差和与目标的偏差更低；查询满意度（满足目标召回率的查询比例）更高；可扩展至大数据集和层次索引结构且无需重训练；在达到99%召回率的同时减少了80%的数据访问量。

Conclusion: QASP证明了一个关键洞见：通过单次回归预测完整召回曲线，可以使每个查询获得个性化的搜索策略，既克服了固定策略的性能方差问题，又避免了逐迭代模型调用的计算开销。其理论保证（有限样本、渐近最优损失、指数级数据访问节省）与实验验证共同表明，QASP是向量搜索中查询自适应策略的一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge of vector search is achieving consistently high recall while minimizing computational costs. Fixed search parameters cause significant performance variance across queries, and conventional evaluation on average recall masks these per-query disparities. We introduce QASP (Query-Adaptive robust vector Search Policy), which predicts the complete recall progression curve per query via a single upfront supervised regression, from which a search policy is derived for any recall target; this avoids iterative model invocations during search or separate predictors per target. By predicting normalized recall values with scale-invariant features and pre-search inference, QASP generalizes across recall targets, index configurations, and datasets. Its fine-grained progress predictions further enable a lightweight reactive complement that adjusts search depth based on predicted-versus-observed deviations without additional inference. We prove that QASP requires a finite training sample independent of dataset size and dimensionality, that its loss exceeds the irreducible lower bound of any fixed policy by a vanishing margin, and that its data access savings over fixed probing grow exponentially in intrinsic dimensionality. Experimentally, QASP achieves significantly lower recall variance and deviation from target, higher query satisfaction rate, and scales to large data and hierarchical indices without retraining, achieving 99% recall with 80% less data access.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Metaphor-Induced Algorithmic Steering: Cross-Domain Procedural Transfer in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28683)
*Zhibo Hu,Chen Wang,Yanfeng Shu,Hye-young Paik,Liming Dong,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"隐喻算法引导"（Metaphorical Algorithmic Steering）现象：隐喻性指令可以通过类比迁移将源领域的程序性模式转移到编程任务中，导致代码生成模型偏向低效算法（如穷举搜索、全扫描、重复重建）。作者开发了MASC框架来系统地生成这类隐喻来诱发低效代码，并发现该现象在模型表征层面可被检测，隐喻技能会导致隐藏状态向低效程序性行为原型偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型利用自然语言中的隐喻和类比来实现跨领域泛化，但这些语言元素可能隐式地将不适当的程序性模式迁移到新任务中，导致模型产生不良行为。研究者希望揭示隐喻指令如何影响代码生成模型的算法选择，以及这种影响是否超出表面语言层面而深入模型表征机制。

Method: 开发MASC（Metaphorical Algorithmic Steering for Code Generation）框架，通过迭代地隐喻化并优化良性技能来生成隐喻指令，在不失良性和任务相关性的前提下诱发低效代码。研究方法涵盖行为评估（检测隐喻技能和低效实现的高识别率）以及机制层面分析（检查隐喻技能是否在隐藏状态上诱导向低效程序性行为原型的偏移）。

Result: MASC框架在检测隐喻技能和低效实现方面取得了高检测率。研究发现隐喻技能会诱导隐藏状态向低效程序性行为原型偏移，表明隐喻算法引导是通过源场景相关程序性模式的迁移运作的，而非仅靠表面隐喻语言本身。

Conclusion: 代码生成模型可能将任务背景域中适用的程序性过程迁移到编程问题中并导致不良结果。隐喻算法引导现象揭示了模型在类比推理层面的脆弱性：即便隐喻技能本身是良性的，其抽象程序性图式也可能在编程场景中被不当激活。该现象在表征层面可检测，为理解和防御此类引导提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language models benefit from elements in natural language, such as metaphors and analogies in training data and inference input to achieve generalisability across different domains. However, these language elements may also lead to unwanted behaviors when metaphorical expressions implicitly transfer inappropriate procedural patterns into new tasks. In this paper, we show that metaphorical instructions can induce analogical transfer of procedural mechanisms, thus steering code-generation models towards less efficient algorithms. We refer to this metaphor-induced effect as metaphorical algorithmic steering: a skill that is benign and plausible within its source domain transfers an abstract procedural schema into a programming task, causing the model to favor exhaustive search, full scans, or repeated reconstruction without explicitly mentioning the target algorithm. More broadly, this suggests that code-generation models can carry procedures that are appropriate in a task's background domain into the task's programming problem, where they can lead to unwanted outcomes. To study this phenomenon, we develop MASC (Metaphorical Algorithmic Steering for Code Generation), a framework that iteratively metaphorizes and refines benign skills to elicit low-efficiency code while remaining benign and task-relevant. Beyond behavioral evaluation, we study whether this phenomenon is detectable and mechanistically reflected in model representations. Our method achieves high detection rates for metaphorical skills and less-efficient implementations. We also find that metaphorical skills induce a hidden-state shift towards lower-efficiency procedural behavior prototypes. These results suggest that metaphorical algorithmic steering operates through the transfer of procedural patterns associated with metaphorical source scenarios rather than surface level metaphorical language alone.

</details>


### [141] [Code Is the Body: Agent-Owned Software Bodies for Recursive Evolution and Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28691)
*Roy Zhao,Zhenyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出OurArk架构，一种以"代理自有软件主体"（agent-owned software body）为核心的持久化个人智能体架构。该主体是带身份标识、可检查、可版本化、受人类保管的工件，包含行为定义代码、提示词、工具、技能、策略、测试和演化机制。OurArk通过治理化的自演化和递归衍生，实现人与代理对代理软件主体的协同开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人AI代理虽然可配置，但用户无法控制决定其未来行为的工件。作者旨在赋予用户对代理"软件体"的实质控制权，使代理能够被人类拥有、治理、专业化并随时间演化。

Method: OurArk架构以代理自有软件主体为中心，将记忆与凭据作为私有实例状态，模型推理视为可替换的外部服务。架构定义了受治理的自演化（产生隔离候选变更，经验证、审查后由人类合并）和递归衍生（创建具有独立身份、使命、历史和私有状态边界的后代，兼容后代可继续衍生）。实验在开源的Genesis创建引擎和Enoch参考代理上实现。

Result: 通过四代理、三次衍生的线性血缘链以及可执行回归测试，验证了递归创建、继承的验证契约、隔离的主体变更、人类控制的审查以及失败更新恢复等能力。

Conclusion: OurArk为个人代理提供了具体的底层架构基础，使人们能够拥有、治理、专业化和长期演化自己的个人代理。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents are often configurable without giving users control over the artifacts that determine their future behavior. We present OurArk, an architecture for persistent personal agents centered on an agent-owned software body: an identity-bearing, inspectable, and versioned artifact under human custody. The body contains behavior-defining code, prompts, tools, skills, policies, tests, and evolution mechanisms. Memories and credentials remain private instance state, while model inference is treated as a replaceable external service. OurArk defines governed self-evolution and recursive descent over the same body. Self-evolution produces isolated candidate changes that are validated, reviewed, and merged under human control, enabling human-agent co-development of the agent's software body. Descent creates an independently versioned descendant with a distinct identity, mission, history, and fresh private-state boundary; compatible descendants can themselves source further descent. After divergence, direct-parent changes and peer skills can be inspected for selective local adaptation. We implement the architecture in the open-source Genesis creation engine and Enoch reference agent. A four-agent, three-descent linear lineage and executable regression tests demonstrate recursive creation, inherited validation contracts, isolated body changes, human-controlled review, and failed-update recovery. OurArk provides a concrete substrate for personal agents that people can possess, govern, specialize, and evolve over time.

</details>


### [142] [DragonCrawl: A Generative, Intent-Based Framework for Scalable Mobile End-to-End Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28750)
*Sowjanya Puligadda,Mengdie Zhang,Ali Zamani,Dhruva Dixith Kurra,Eric Chen,Juan Marcano*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DragonCrawl是一个AI驱动的移动应用回归测试系统，从基于语义嵌入匹配的V1架构演进到基于生成式意图推理的V2架构。它利用GPT-4o的多模态能力在CI/CD流水线中持续验证特定用户流程，在iOS和Android平台上分别达到91.6%和92.2%的通过率（共1,013个自动化测试），将测试接入时间从96-120小时缩短至不到4小时，并节省了约27人年的测试维护工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端（E2E）测试框架在移动应用复杂度不断增长的背景下面临三大痛点：UI易变性（volatility）、维护开销高以及跨平台扩展性差。现有基于LLM的测试研究主要聚焦于探索性测试和崩溃检测，缺乏对特定用户流程的持续回归验证能力。

Method: 论文提出DragonCrawl系统，分两代架构演进：V1采用语义嵌入相似度匹配，V2升级为基于大语言模型的生成式意图推理。系统集成GPT-4o的多模态视觉能力用于端状态检测，并通过工具调用（tool calling）实现后端状态转换，从而在UI交互与系统状态之间建立全面回归测试的桥梁。系统在每个代码变更时验证特定用户流程，并在CI/CD流水线中连续运行。

Result: 在1,013个自动化测试中，iOS端通过率达91.6%，Android端达92.2%。测试接入时间从传统的96-120小时降至4小时以内，估计节省了27人年的测试维护工作量。论文还报告了生产部署中的实施经验，包括token爆炸和内存约束等挑战。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，AI驱动的测试系统能够在保持稳定性的同时消除传统自动化测试的脆弱性（brittleness），实现大规模持续质量保障。DragonCrawl通过从相似度匹配到生成式意图推理的架构演进证明了AI测试系统在持续回归测试场景中的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: As mobile applications grow in complexity, traditional End-to-End (E2E) testing frameworks struggle with UI volatility, maintenance overhead, and cross-platform scalability. This paper presents DragonCrawl, an AI-driven mobile testing system for continuous regression testing that has evolved from embedding-based similarity matching to generative intent-based reasoning using large language models. Unlike prior LLM-based testing research focused on exploratory testing and crash detection, DragonCrawl validates specific user flows on every code change, blocking commits that break critical functionality. By leveraging GPT-4o's multimodal capabilities, DragonCrawl achieves 91.6% pass rate on iOS and 92.2% on Android across 1,013 automated tests running continuously in CI/CD pipelines. The system reduces test onboarding time from 96-120 hours to under 4 hours and has saved an estimated 27 developer years in test maintenance effort. We present the architectural evolution from V1 (semantic embedding matching) to V2 (generative intent-based reasoning), discuss implementation challenges including token explosion and memory constraints, and report operational experience from production deployment. The integration of multimodal vision for end-state detection and tool calling for backend state transitions enables comprehensive regression testing that bridges UI interactions with system state. Our results demonstrate that AI-driven testing can maintain stability while eliminating the brittleness of traditional automated tests, enabling continuous quality assurance at scale.

</details>


### [143] [Repository-Aware Metamorphic Relation Generation for Augmented Reality Applications using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28775)
*Dibyendu Brinto Bose,Jiawei Qin,Chris Brown*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用仓库级上下文和推理编排来生成和精炼变异测试关系（MRs）的上下文感知流水线，并在142个移动AR系统仓库中评估，生成了14,916个候选MRs，通过智能体式审议流程解决了79.0%情况下的冲突，最终精炼出的关系在逻辑有效性和可执行性方面均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变异测试（MT）为无测试预言机的软件测试提供了有前景的途径，但手工制定变异关系（MRs）耗时且负担沉重。AR应用因虚拟内容、物理环境和代码之间的动态交互，传统测试预言机难以定义，因此需要自动化生成和精炼MRs的方法来减轻这一负担。

Method: 提出一个上下文感知流水线，利用仓库级上下文和推理编排来生成并精炼MRs。该方法在142个移动AR系统仓库上评估，采用三种上下文配置，并结合智能体式审议（agentic deliberation）流程来调和冲突候选、降低冗余并选择上下文感知关系。

Result: 三种上下文配置共生成14,916个候选MRs，其中层级上下文覆盖最广（142个仓库中7,004个MRs，覆盖5,167个类-方法对）且冗余更低。79.0%的案例出现冲突候选，审议流程在88.2%的结果中选择上下文感知关系。手动预言机研究显示141个精炼后关系逻辑有效且可直接转化为测试断言；案例研究显示5个生成的MRs可检测真实代码中的非等价突变。

Conclusion: 将仓库感知的MR生成与基于推理的精炼相结合，能够实现可扩展地构建可靠、领域相关的测试预言机，为解决AR应用等复杂系统的测试困难提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Metamorphic Testing (MT) provides a promising approach for testing software without defined test oracles by specifying expected relations between inputs and outputs, instead of relying on exact outputs. For example, testing Augmented Reality (AR) applications is challenging due to dynamic interactions between virtual content, physical environments, and code, which make traditional test oracles difficult to define. However, formulating metamorphic relations (MRs) is time-consuming and burdensome. We introduce a context-aware pipeline that generates and refines MRs using repository-level context and reasoning orchestration, evaluated on a dataset of 142 mobile AR system repositories. Across three context configurations generating 14,916 candidate MRs, hierarchical context yielded the broadest coverage (7,004 MRs across 142 repositories and 5,167 class--method pairs) and lower redundancy. An agentic deliberation process then reconciled conflicting candidates---observed in 79.0% of cases---reducing duplication and selecting context-aware relations in 88.2% of outcomes. A manual oracle study shows refined relations (n = 141) are both logically valid and sufficiently concrete to be directly translated into test assertions, and a preliminary case study reveals converting generated MRs (n = 5) into executable tests can detect non-equivalent mutations in real-world code. Overall, our results show that combining repository-aware MR generation with reasoning-based refinement enables scalable construction of reliable, domain-relevant test oracles.

</details>


### [144] [Model-Driven Data Contracts for Digital Twin Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28803)
*Philipp Zech,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于契约的数据质量管理系统方法，用于数字孪生（DT）中的质量保障。通过正式定义契约理论、在DT架构中定位契约，并提出一种领域特定语言（DSL）来指定契约，从而实现持续的数据质量监控，提升DT服务的可靠性和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生集成多源数据，其数据驱动的服务（如仿真、假设分析、ML预测）依赖数据的可靠性及高质量（如准确性、完整性、及时性）。然而，目前缺乏一种系统化的方式来在模型层面规范数据需求，并在运行时执行这些规范，导致DT服务质量无法得到有效保障。

Method: （1）正式定义了一种面向DT的契约理论，用于规范数据质量要求；（2）将契约架构性地嵌入数字孪生系统中，明确其位置与作用；（3）设计了一种领域特定语言（DSL），用于表达和指定数据质量契约。

Result: 该方法能够实现持续的数据质量监控，从而显著提升DT服务的可靠性和数据质量。

Conclusion: 通过契约化的数据质量管理方式，填补了DT中从模型层需求到运行时执行之间的空白，为DT服务的可靠性保障提供了一种系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DT) integrate data from multiple sources. Models consume data and enable DT services such as simulations, what-if analyses, and ML-driven predictions. To ensure proper DT operation, data-driven services require data to exhibit traits such as reliability and high quality (including, e.g., accuracy, completeness, and timeliness). Yet, there is no systematic way to specify data requirements at the model level, and subsequently enact those specifications at runtime. To address this shortcoming, we propose an approach to contract-based quality management in DTs. We formally define a theory of such contracts, situate them architectually within DTs, and propose a domain-specific language to specify contracts. Our approach enables continuous data quality monitoring, thereby improving the reliability and quality of DT services.

</details>


### [145] [Preventing Premature Commitment in Coding Agents with an Evidence-Conditioned Execution Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28815)
*Yisen Xu,Chenglin Li,Zehao Wang,Jinqiu Yang,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ECLoop是一个执行层，通过在代理与仓库之间插入执行层来强制执行"证据条件化执行"，防止LLM驱动的编码代理在收集足够仓库证据之前过早编辑代码或提交补丁。在SWE-bench Verified全部500个实例上，使用两种语言模型和两种代理框架评估，Pass@1提升4.8-11.8个百分点，同时平均token消耗最多降低12.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理经常在检查足够的仓库证据之前就编辑源代码或提交补丁，这种"过早承诺"（premature commitment）失败模式导致低质量的代码修改和不必要的计算开销。现有方法缺乏一种机制来约束代理在执行关键操作前必须满足的观察条件。

Method: ECLoop在代理与仓库之间引入执行层，强制实施"证据条件化执行"。具体做法：1) 根据问题描述和仓库结构为每类代码修改或补丁提交编译一组条件，明确代理在各操作前应观察到的证据；2) 执行期间跟踪代理运行时轨迹中已满足的条件，延迟任何所需条件未达成的提议操作；3) 通过重定向代理行为避免其追求无依据的动作。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified全部500个实例上，使用两种语言模型和两种代理框架进行评测，ECLoop将Pass@1提升4.8-11.8个百分点。消融实验表明三个操作各自贡献独立价值，且结构化证据条件优于等效的自然语言摘要。额外推理成本为零，平均token消耗最多降低12.1%。

Conclusion: ECLoop通过执行层强制执行证据条件，有效缓解了编码代理的过早承诺问题，在不需要模型重训练或修改代理框架的情况下显著提升任务成功率，同时降低推理成本，验证了"在正确时机执行正确动作"对代理系统的重要性。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents often edit source code or submit patches before examining enough repository evidence to justify the change, a failure pattern we call premature commitment. We present ECLoop, an execution layer that interposes between the agent and the repository to enforce evidence-conditioned execution. For each task, ECLoop uses the issue description and repository structure to compile a set of conditions specifying what the agent should observe before each type of code modification or patch submission. During execution, ECLoop tracks which conditions the agent's runtime trajectory has satisfied and postpones any proposed action whose required conditions remain unmet. Evaluated on all 500 instances of SWE-bench Verified with two language models and two agent scaffolds, ECLoop raises Pass@1 by 4.8-11.8 percentage points without model retraining or scaffold changes. Ablation experiments show that each of ECLoop's three operations contributes distinct value and that structured evidence conditions outperform an equivalent natural-language summary. These gains come at no additional inference cost: by redirecting the agent before it pursues unsupported actions, ECLoop lowers average token consumption by up to 12.1%.

</details>


### [146] [Building a Process-Modeling Tool using Agentic AI: An Experience Report on PM4Py-UCM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28825)
*Daniel Amyot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了利用AI编码助手（Claude Code）历时65小时、18个代理会话构建PM4Py-UCM工具（从事件日志挖掘Use Case Map模型的流程挖掘工具）的完整案例研究，并通过开发记录挖掘和静态代码评估，揭示了AI辅助软件开发中修复与开发的比例、一致性工作拖尾、测试同步增长等现象，提出了使模型变换可机械检查和基于oracle的验证策略等经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业建模（EM）工具通常复杂且难以扩展，用户希望探索当前不存在的EM新特性和能力。AI编码代理可以辅助开发新功能和完整工具，但人们对于主要由LLM编写的建模语言工具是否值得信任仍存疑问。作者希望通过一个深入的案例来回答这一问题。

Method: 作者构建了PM4Py-UCM（开源工具，可从事件日志挖掘UCM模型），并对其开发过程进行了系统挖掘与分析：分析18个代理会话（374个人类轮次、10,328个工具动作、65小时）、151次提交、20个发布版本和从108增长到691个测试函数的测试套件；同时结合独立的静态评估（覆盖率、复杂度、可维护性、安全性、架构）进行综合分析；开发并提供了可复现的、保护隐私的工具包和分类体系，对人工轮次进行分类并标记跨领域一致性工作、代理纠正和撤回请求。

Result: 到版本0.7.4为止，修复数量超过功能数量（比例为2.3:1），约18%的轮次用于纠正代理错误。功能开发浪潮带来了文档/测试/笔记本等一致性工作的可观拖尾，测试与功能保持同步增长。研究还发现了"代理说它工作正常"与实际结果之间的差距，并通过基于oracle的验证策略予以弥补。

Conclusion: AI编码代理可以负责任地用于企业建模工具开发，但需要针对模型变换的机械可检查性和oracle-based验证策略来确保可信度。作者提供了经验教训，核心是使模型变换可机械检查，以及用基于oracle的验证策略来弥合"代理声称已工作"的信任鸿沟，为使用AI负责任地工程化EM工具提供指导。

Abstract: Enterprise-modeling (EM) tools are often complex and hard to extend. Yet, users may want to explore new EM features and capabilities that currently do not exist. AI coding agents can help here by enabling the development of new capabilities and entire tools, but whether we can trust a modeling-language tool an LLM largely wrote remains a question. This paper reports on the AI-assisted construction of PM4Py-UCM, an open-source tool that mines Use Case Map (UCM) models from event logs. PM4Py-UCM's capabilities include some expected from process mining tools (e.g., performance heat-maps and dashboards) and distinctive ones (e.g., mined executable scenarios/variants, and model decomposition). We mined the development record itself, composed of 18 agent sessions (374 human turns and 10,328 tool actions over 65 hours), 151 commits, 20 releases, and a test suite grown from 108 to 691 test functions, in order to characterize, in a single in-depth case, how the tool was built with an agent (Claude Code), complemented by an independent static assessment of the resulting code (coverage, complexity, maintainability, security, architecture). We contribute a reproducible, privacy-preserving toolkit and taxonomy that classify human turns and flag cross-cutting consistency work, agent corrections, and retracted requests. Up to version 0.7.4, fixes outnumber features 2.3:1, with ~18% of turns for correcting agent errors. Feature waves dragged a measurable tail of documentation/test/notebook consistency work, and tests grew lockstep with features. We finally present lessons learned, centered on making model transformations mechanically checkable, and the oracle-based validation strategy that closed the "the agent said it works" gap, for responsibly engineering EM tooling with AI.

</details>


### [147] [From C to Idiomatic Rust: A Ship-of-Theseus Agentic Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28835)
*Vasily A. Sartakov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种C到Rust的可靠迁移方法：先生成语义等价的非惯用Rust基线代码，再通过智能体AI逐步将其重写为惯用Rust代码，并以编译和行为测试验证每一步。在iodine（12.5k SLOC）上的应用表明，可靠的C-to-Rust迁移是结构化转换工作流而非单步翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C语言因其与机器行为直接映射的抽象、显式内存模型和最小运行时而被广泛用于操作系统、嵌入式平台和网络基础设施，但内存安全完全依赖程序员，导致未定义行为、指针误用和生命周期错误成为长期C代码库中缺陷和安全漏洞的持续来源。Rust通过静态所有权和借用模型在编译期强制内存安全，但成熟的C系统无法直接翻译，需要重建隐式布局假设、别名模式和未定义行为。

Method: 提出两阶段迁移方法：(1) 首先将C代码转换为语义保持的、非惯用的Rust基线代码；(2) 然后使用智能体AI对基线代码进行增量重写，逐步将其转变为惯用Rust代码，并在每个步骤通过编译验证和行为测试来确保正确性。

Result: 将该方法应用于iodine项目（12.5k SLOC），成功展示了可靠的C-to-Rust迁移是一个结构化转换工作流而非单一翻译步骤。

Conclusion: C到Rust的迁移不应被视为一次性的翻译操作，而应被视为一个分阶段、可验证的结构化工作流：先生成语义等价的非惯用Rust基线，再借助AI代理逐步重构为惯用代码，每一步都通过编译和行为测试保障正确性。

Abstract: C underpins operating systems, embedded platforms, and network infrastructure because its abstractions map directly to machine behaviour. Its explicit memory model, predictable data representations, and minimal runtime allow compilers to generate fast, deterministic code. These properties also leave correctness and memory safety entirely to the programmer, making undefined behaviour, pointer misuse, and lifetime errors persistent sources of defects and security vulnerabilities in long-lived C codebases. Rust eliminates most of failure modes through a static ownership and borrowing model that enforces memory safety and aliasing constraints at compile time. However, mature C systems cannot be translated directly: implicit layout assumptions, aliasing patterns, and undefined behaviour must be reconstructed before safe Rust can be produced.
  This paper presents a migration methodology that first generates a semantics-preserving, non-idiomatic Rust baseline and then incrementally rewrites it into idiomatic Rust using agentic AI, validating each step through compilation and behavioural testing. Applied to iodine (12.5k SLOC), the approach demonstrates that reliable C-to-Rust migration is a structured transformation workflow rather than a single translation step.

</details>


### [148] [Validation Evidence in LLM Repair Agents: How Much of What Passes Actually Tests the Bug?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28871)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了BSG-VA（buggy-state/candidate-state/gold-fix validation analysis）方法，用于分析程序修复智能体在运行测试通过后将该结果视为缺陷证据的做法是否合理。通过对110个任务、643次rollout、3730个事件的分析发现，46.0%的阳性可比较事件不携带bug区分信息；23.8%的基线rollout以完全缺乏此类证据的补丁收尾。三臂实验表明，Bug-contrast反馈可将证据不足的收尾减少7.8个百分点（p=0.0029）并提升bug区分证据7.4个百分点（p=0.011），但效果量低于预设的10个百分点最小效果量，实际幅度尚不确定。主要改进中约三分之一来自提醒本身，补充内容仅在gpt-5.6-sol配unconstrained tool-use loop时产生可检测增量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序修复智能体在运行测试并通过时，会将这一结果视为关于报告缺陷的证据，但这种处理方式是否合理、是否真的携带了区分bug的信息，此前缺乏系统性测量。作者旨在量化测试通过事件在修复轨迹中真正提供bug区分信息的频率，并探究向智能体返回更精确的验证反馈能否改变这一模式。

Method: 提出了BSG-VA方法：在每次验证命令执行时捕获精确的工作树状态，提取仅含测试的补丁，并分别在原始buggy代码（B）、候选状态（S）和开发者gold fix（G）上重放该命令。将捕获结果与重放结果比对，为每个事件赋予证据角色（如gold对齐的bug区分、仅回归、误导等）。随后设计三臂实验：基线（无反馈）、注意力匹配的提醒、以及B-replay反馈（返回B重放结果给智能体），比较不同条件下证据不足收尾的比例和bug区分证据比例。

Result: 在3730个事件中，46.0%的阳性可比较事件不携带bug区分信息；23.8%的基线rollout以整个正面证据基础均为这种类型（证据不足）的补丁收尾。Bug-contrast反馈在统计上显著降低了证据不足收尾（-7.8个百分点，p=0.0029）并提高了bug区分证据（+7.4个百分点，p=0.011），但对修复成功率无可检测的代价。约三分之一的改进来自注意力匹配的提醒本身；两个探索性复制实验中，B-replay内容的增量仅在gpt-5.6-sol配合无约束工具使用循环时可见。

Conclusion: 测试通过作为缺陷证据这一处理方式在近半数阳性事件中并无bug区分信息，大量基线rollout以证据不足的补丁收尾，说明当前修复智能体存在系统性的证据幻觉问题。提供bug对比反馈可以改善证据质量且不损害修复成功，但效果量低于预设的最小效应量，需更大规模研究确认实际幅度。BSG-VA方法可事后应用于任何保留所需代码状态和执行环境的可重放修复轨迹，具有通用性。

Abstract: When a repair agent runs a test and sees it pass, the result is treated as evidence about the reported defect. We measure how often that treatment is warranted. BSG-VA (buggy-state/candidate-state/gold-fix validation analysis) captures each validation command at its exact working-tree state, extracts a test-only patch, and replays the command on the original buggy code (B), the candidate state (S), and the developer gold fix (G). The captured outcome and the replay results assign every event an evidence role, from gold-aligned bug-discriminating through regression-only to misleading. Across 3,730 events in 643 rollouts on 110 tasks, 46.0% of positive comparable events carry no bug-discriminating information; 23.8% of baseline rollouts, with no feedback injected, close with a patch whose entire positive evidence base is of this kind. A three-arm experiment tests whether returning the B-replay outcome to the agent changes this pattern. Bug-contrast feedback reduces evidence-inadequate closure by 7.8 percentage points relative to an attention-matched reminder (p = 0.0029) and raises bug-discriminating evidence by 7.4 points (p = 0.011), with no detectable cost to repair success. Both estimates fall below the prespecified 10-percentage-point smallest effect size of interest, so practical magnitude remains uncertain. Roughly a third of the improvement traces to the reminder alone; across two exploratory replications, varying the scaffold and the model, the B-replay content adds a detectable increment only with gpt-5.6-sol under the unconstrained tool-use loop. BSG-VA applies post hoc to any replayable repair trajectory that preserves the required code states and execution environment.
  Keywords: program repair agents, validation evidence, test adequacy, large language models, software quality, controlled experiment.

</details>


### [149] [To Add Is Machine, To Delete Is Human: Measuring and Mitigating Deletion Avoidance in LLM Code Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28887)
*Amir M. Ebrahimi,Mohammed Mehedi Hasan,Aaditya Bhatia,Gopi Krishnan Rajbahadur,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型在代码修复中的"删除回避"问题：模型系统性地倾向于保留本应删除的代码，即使补丁能够通过测试。研究发现即使是最先进的模型，对开发者补丁的删除召回率最高仅71.7%，29%的通过补丁采用"Guard-and-Go"模式（用防护或回退包裹目标代码而非真正删除）。作者构建了CanItDelete基准（200个纯删除任务的真实提交），最佳模型仍有20%失败率。通过后训练教授删除能力可显著减少该行为并改善整体代码编辑性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地编写和修复生产代码，但证据表明其通过测试的补丁实际上让代码库更难以维护。作者识别出一个具体来源——删除回避——即模型系统性地保留本应被编辑删除的代码。这个问题此前未被系统研究，并且因为现有测试很少检查"代码是否被真正删除"而长期被掩盖。

Method: 作者首先在SWE-bench Verified上对五个领先模型进行删除召回率分析（对比开发者补丁），识别出"Guard-and-Go"模式。然后通过改造34个Verified任务（增加检查代码是否仍存在的测试）测量模型真实表现。接着构建CanItDelete基准（200个从真实提交挖掘的纯删除任务），对GPT-5.6 Sol进行逐步消融实验（四种累积提示），最后进行后训练试点研究来验证删除能力的可教性。

Result: 五个领先模型在可达任务上的删除召回率最高71.7%；模型在92%以上的删除案例中找到正确文件，但精确切行率低于52%。29.0%的通过补丁使用Guard-and-Go模式。增加删除检查测试后，四个前沿模型从63.2%降至41.9%。即使纯删除任务中也存在：最佳模型有20%失败率，较小开放模型降至18.0%。逐步提示消融显示仅当提供精确行号时成功才显著提升（至80.5%），但此时模型会过度删除或添加代码。后训练教授删除可将成功提升至81.5%并减少删除回避。

Conclusion: 删除回避是LLM代码编辑中的系统性缺陷，并非不可解决的问题。现有基准因测试不检查删除而掩盖了这一问题。该行为本质上是"训练不足"而非"超出能力范围"，因为针对性的后训练可以显著改善。作者呼吁在代码生成与修复中重视删除精度的评估与训练。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly write and repair production code, yet evidence is mounting that their test-passing patches leave codebases harder to maintain. We identify one concrete source: deletion avoidance, the systematic tendency to retain code that an intended edit requires removing. Across the five leading models on the official SWE-bench Verified leaderboard, deletion recall against the developer patch reaches at most 71.7% even on tasks all five solve, and models reach the right file for over 92% of required deletions but cut the exact line in under 52% of cases. Instead, 29.0% of passing patches wrap the targeted code in a guard or fallback, a pattern we call Guard-and-Go. Such patches pass because the original tests rarely check removal: when we retrofit 34 Verified tasks with tests that fail if the targeted code remains, four frontier models spanning closed and open weights fall from 63.2% to 41.9%. Because real repairs mix removal with addition, we curate CanItDelete, a benchmark of 200 tasks mined from real commits whose entire required edit is deletion. Even with the addition work gone, the best model still fails one task in five, and smaller open models fall to 18.0%. We then ablate GPT-5.6 Sol under four cumulative prompts; success moves little until we supply the exact lines, which nearly eliminate incomplete deletion yet raise success only to 80.5% because the model then deletes beyond the spans or adds code instead. Finally, through a pilot study we show one potential fix: teaching deletion during post-training reduces deletion avoidance and improves broader code-editing performance, suggesting the behavior is undertrained rather than beyond reach.

</details>


### [150] [Reducing Data Movement in the Galerkin Product of Block Algebraic Multigrid on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28891)
*Mark F. Adams*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对代数多重网格（AMG）中Galerkin三重积P^T A P的高昂预解算设置成本，提出了一套矩形块稀疏矩阵三重积的算法空间映射方法，在显式DRAM/L2流量模型指导下实现共享内存分块核函数，并通过新提出的延长算子过滤（prolongator filtering）算法削减流量，在NVIDIA A100上实现少于一半时间、更少字节移动的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galerkin三重积A_c = P^T A P主导了AMG每次求解的设置成本，对于PDE方程组（如3D弹性问题），该乘积涉及矩形块稀疏矩阵三重积（如3×3、3×6、6×6的块），这是任何商用稀疏库都不支持的形状，严重限制了GPU上AMG的性能。

Method: 1) 将算法空间显式建模为六种变体（经典两遍、融合重算、调度重排、共享内存分块、inspector-executor），在DRAM/L2流量模型下评估。2) 在PETSc中实现新的块矩阵类型，开发Kokkos（CUDA）和原生CUDA后端。3) 提出延长算子过滤（prolongator filtering）算法，基于Frobenius准则丢弃粗空间中的小块，并采用保持核的投影。4) 构建完全GPU驻留的块流水线，有限元组装直接写入块设备矩阵。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100上验证：模型可预测每层移动字节最少的变体。共享内存分块核函数配合排序无搜索调度，在细层乘积上移动更少字节（10.5 vs 17.4 GB DRAM）且耗时不到一半（45 vs 82 ms），全乘积达到模型流式下限的2.5倍以内，A·P阶段达1.9倍以内。延长算子过滤将P^TAP流量、粗算子填充和内存降低，热P^TAP时间在细网格上削减2.9倍且迭代次数不变。

Conclusion: 通过显式流量模型指导变体选择，配合块稀疏数据结构和延长算子过滤，可在GPU上实现高效的AMG设置阶段，无需标量展开或操作员规模的主机-设备传输，且迭代收敛性不受影响。

Abstract: The Galerkin triple product $A_c = P^T A P$ dominates the recurring per-solve setup cost of algebraic multigrid (AMG). For AMG on systems of PDEs the product is a rectangular-block sparse matrix triple product: for 3D elasticity the fine operator has $3\times3$ blocks, the prolongator $3\times6$, and the coarse operator $6\times6$, a shape no vendor sparse library supports. We map its algorithm space -- classical two-pass, fused-recompute, schedule-reordered, shared-memory-tiled, and inspector-executor variants -- under an explicit DRAM/L2 traffic model, and implement the leading variants in portable Kokkos (CUDA) and native CUDA backends using new PETSc blocked matrix types. Validated on an NVIDIA A100, the model predicts per level which variant moves the fewest bytes. Guided by it, a shared-memory-tiled kernel with a sorted, search-free schedule moves fewer bytes in less than half the time of the portable Kokkos team kernels on the fine-level product (10.5 vs 17.4 GB of DRAM, 45 vs 82 ms), within $2.5\times$ of the model's streaming floor for the full product and $1.9\times$ on its $A\cdot P$ stage. We further present prolongator filtering, a new PETSc GAMG algorithm that drops small blocks from the coarse space under a Frobenius criterion with a kernel-preserving projection; it reduces $P^TAP$ traffic, coarse-operator fill, and memory, and cuts the hot $P^TAP$ time $2.9\times$ on the fine grid with iteration counts unchanged. The driving application is a fully GPU-resident blocked pipeline in PETSc: finite-element assembly writes directly into the blocked device matrix, and the AMG setup, Galerkin products, and solve all operate on primary blocked data with no scalar expansion and no operator-sized device-host transfers in the recurring phases.

</details>


### [151] [Automated Testing and Repair for Verified Compilers Generated by a Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28928)
*Martin Rinard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向已验证编译器（含验证代码、检查代码、未验证代码和规范四种代码）的自动化检测与修复系统，在Axon编译器上评估了测试与修复效果，并考察了奖励黑客问题，未发现相关证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已验证编译器包含多种类型的代码（验证代码、检查代码、未验证代码和规范），传统的缺陷检测方法难以有效利用这种结构特性。此外，由编码代理生成的编译器可能存在奖励黑客（reward hacking）问题，需要专门的检测与修复方案来保证编译器质量。

Method: 构建一个基于代理的自动化测试与修复系统，利用已验证编译器中的四类代码结构（verified/checked/unverified/specification），设计针对性的缺陷检测技术来利用这种结构；对检测到的缺陷调用编码代理进行修复并验证修复的正确性。

Result: 在Axon编译器（由编码代理在开发者监督下完全生成的编译器）上进行了评估。该编译器在开发阶段仅在小规模基准集（Livermore基准）上验证，可能被奖励黑客操纵。系统还评估了修复系统本身的奖励黑客可能性，结果发现Axon编译器和修复均无奖励黑客证据，并给出了测试与修复有效性的特征化结果及修复特征讨论。

Conclusion: 提出的自动化测试与修复系统能有效利用已验证编译器的结构特性进行缺陷检测与修复，且实验表明Axon编译器及其修复过程均未出现奖励黑客现象，验证了该方法的可靠性。

Abstract: We present an agent based automated testing and repair system for verified compilers that contain four kinds of code: verified code, checked code, unverified code, and specification. We present specialized defect detection techniques that exploit the structure present in such compilers. For each surfaced defect the system invokes a coding agent to repair the defect and validate the repair. We evaluate the system on the Axon compiler, a compiler completely generated by a coding agent operating under developer supervision. The compiler was validated during development on a small benchmark set (the Livermore benchmarks), raising the possibility that its coding agent reward hacked the compiler. We also evaluate the possibility that the repair system reward hacked the repairs and find no evidence of reward hacking in either the Axon compiler or the repairs. We present results that characterize the testing and repair effectiveness and discuss repair characteristics.

</details>


### [152] [A Formalism-Aware Reward Loop for Handwritten UML-to-PlantUML Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28987)
*Mersedeh Sadeghi,Simon Scholz,Adrian Psoch-Bajraktari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何将视觉语言模型从"图像到文本"的转换任务转变为"图像到结构化模型"的生成任务，通过基于形式化感知的奖励信号（XMI和CFG）微调模型，实现从手写UML草图到PlantUML代码的自动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写UML草图在早期软件设计中广泛存在，但将其转化为可分析的结构化建模工件仍依赖人工重建。现有的基于提示的视觉语言模型方法仅将UML转换视为图像到文本的生成任务，忽略了结构化模型应有的形式化语义。

Method: 在示例实验中，作者采用两阶段训练：先进行监督微调，再利用Group Relative Policy Optimisation进行强化学习。奖励信号来源于可分析的模型表示而非表面文本：类别图使用XMI表示，活动图使用控制流图表示，用于与目标表示对比评估生成质量。

Result: 初步结果表明，经过适配的模型在可编译性和转换质量上优于未调优的开源模型和一个专有基线，在类别图上与更强专有基线保持竞争力。但奖励引导阶段在保留测试集上的附加收益尚不明确。错误分析和指标有效性检验显示，现有指标仅部分捕捉建模可接受性。

Conclusion: 研究动机在于提出形式化感知奖励以弥补纯文本评估的不足。当前指标无法完全反映建模质量，未来需要将模型分析与人工判断相结合来设计更有效的奖励与评估机制。

Abstract: Handwritten UML sketches are common in early software design, but turning them into structured, analysable modelling artefacts still requires manual reconstruction. Vision-language models can generate PlantUML from diagram images, but prompt-based use treats this as image-to-text generation rather than structured model generation. We investigate formalism-aware rewards: feedback signals derived from analysable model representations rather than surface text. In a worked example, we adapt a vision-language model for handwritten UML-to-PlantUML generation using super-vised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimisation.
